# FMM-Lille vs our search pipeline

Generated by `eu.solven.matmul.docs.explore.FmmSearchComparison`.
Compares the best rank our search pipeline (Strassen + concat +
Kronecker + recombination + Pan pair-fused, with a formula-aware
SOTA resolver knowing Pan TA / HK formulas) can predict for every
shape ⟨n,m,p⟩ with n ≤ m ≤ p ≤ 32, against fmm-lille's
published best rank for that shape.

**This is the search-level diff** — distinct from
`fmm-lille-discrepancies.md` which compares schemes already
materialised on disk. A GAP here is a *missing technique* in our
pipeline; a TIE means our search would produce a constructive
lineage that matches fmm-lille's rank claim, even if no scheme is
on disk yet.

## Headline counts

| category | count | meaning |
|---|---:|---|
| **WIN** | 858 | our predicted rank `<` fmm-lille — materialise + register |
| **TIE** | 2039 | match fmm-lille, we have a constructive strategy |
| **GAP** | 2529 | fmm-lille beats us — missing technique |
| no FMM  | 30 | we have a strategy, fmm-lille lists nothing |
| no ours | 0 | fmm-lille has it, our search returned empty |
